Let's design algorithm:

- Internal fast score function using FFT for q and max.
```
def fast_score(p):
    if p sum < 1e-12: return inf
    p = p / p.sum()
    q = fft_conv(p,p) # real, length 2n-1
    return 2*n*q.max()
```
Need accurate enough. Use rfft: size next power of two >=2n-1. q=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p,N)**2, N)[:2n-1]. Since nonneg, small negative due roundoff clip.

- For evaluations to global evaluator? We can use fast for search, but final maybe call evaluate_sequence to validate and maybe compare? The evaluator uses np.convolve exact direct; rounding might differ at 1e-15. We can trust fast but perhaps final use evaluate_sequence on best and fallback.

- Smooth objective: `smooth_obj(z, beta)`? Working with p = softmax(z). But softmax each iteration costly O(n). We can maintain p and z? Simpler: use p directly with multiplicative updates preserving positive and normalize. Gradient of g(p)=logsumexp(β q) wrt p = 2β r (where r=sum w p_{k-i}). But moving in p with simplex constraint: use z log update: p ← p * exp(-η * 2β p r)? Wait gradient wrt z_i where z=log p with constraint? If p = softmax(z), dp/dz_i has covariance, not simply p_i. But if we use multiplicative weights on p and normalize, that approximates projected gradient on log p with L1? Let's derive: We minimize g(p) over simplex. Gradient h_i=∂g/∂p_i. Projected gradient: p_new = p - η h, clip, normalize. Multiplicative: p_i *= exp(-η h_i / p_i)? That is gradient in log p: ∂g/∂log p_i = p_i h_i. For h_i = 2β r_i, update z_i -= η*2β p_i r_i. Then p normalized. This is common. It tends to zeros if large. We can reinitialize zeros? But zeros allowed in final; for search keep min.

Alternatively use direct projected gradient with step in p: p_new = clip(p - η h, 0, inf); normalize. This may create zeros but okay. Step size can be backtracked. Gradient h_i can be large for peaks; projected gradient may converge. Since objective homogeneous, p sum=1.

Maybe use a hybrid: multiplicative log updates stable and stay positive. Use adaptive η.

Need compute r_i. For p and softmax weights w (sum1) q. r_i =∑_j p_j w_{i+j}. We can compute via FFT: conv(p[::-1], w) take slice [n-1:2n-1]. w length 2n-1. For r length n. With FFT size T>=3n? Length conv p_rev (n) and w(2n-1) => 3n-2. Could be larger. But w often close to q? Wait r_i uses w same length as q. We can compute direct O(n^2) if n small; for n=2535 direct 6.5M per gradient maybe okay in C with numpy? We can use np.convolve direct for p_rev and w maybe O(n^2) but in C fast for n=2500? ~6M multiply-add, maybe ~0.01s? Actually np.convolve direct C maybe 0.02s. FFT overhead maybe similar. For larger n, FFT.

But for many iterations, O(n log n) better. Compute q via FFT size N2>=2n-1. r via FFT size N3>=3n-2. N3 ~4096 for n=2535, fine. For n=20000, N3=65536, fine. Memory okay.

Let's design a generic optimizer:

`optimize_p(p, n, budget_s, beta_schedule)`:
- p normalized positive.
- Maintain z=log p + offset. But we can update p multiplicatively.
- Set beta starts maybe 10, increases up to 500. For each beta:
   Iterate up to time limit:
      q = conv(p,p) via FFT
      m=max(q); w=softmax(beta*(q-m))
      r = correlation(p_rev, w) via FFT
      log_grad = p * r  (since h=2β r constant)
      # Center log_grad to avoid drift? In log update with normalization, adding constant irrelevant. Use centered = log_grad - mean(log_grad)
      For several step sizes (max_step maybe 1), candidate = p * exp(-η * beta? Actually log_grad already includes p*r; need scale η. Let's define step = p * exp(-η * r? Wait ∂g/∂p_i =2β r_i. ∂g/∂log p_i = p_i * 2β r_i. Multiplicative update with log_vars: z_i ← z_i - step_size * p_i * 2β r_i. Then p_new ∝ p_i * exp(-step_size*2β p_i r_i). The factor β in exponent can be folded into step. Let λ = η*2β. Use p_new = p * exp(-λ p r). Normalize.
      But if β large, λ maybe small. We can line search over λ values (e.g., 0.1,0.01,...) using score (exact max) or soft objective. Accept if exact score improves (or soft). Use exact score to guide to true objective.
      If no improvement, reduce λ or beta.
   Increase beta.
- Use periodic random restarts/perturbations: add multiplicative noise, reoptimize.

Let's design low-dimensional parameterization:

Use variables θ vector length d (e.g., d=8) representing basis functions on x∈[0,1]: basis = [1, x-0.5, (x-0.5)^2, x(1-x), sin(πx), cos(πx), sin(2πx), cos(2πx), ...]. To ensure positivity, use z_i = θ0? Actually p_i = (1 + ∑_{m≥1} θ_m φ_m(x_i)) / sum, and require all values >ε. We can either enforce with barrier or clip after positive. For optimizer, better parameterize log p_i = ∑ θ_m ψ_m(x_i) (or q values). But linear family near positive. We can use z_i = θ0 + ∑ θ_m φ_m(x_i), p=exp(z)/sum. To maintain broad values, choose z = c + small θ. Starting c=0. This allows any shape but constrained smooth. We can optimize θ with L-BFGS finite differences. Since p=exp(z)/sum, sum invariant omitted; set θ0=0 perhaps to fix scale? Sum not needed because softmax; θ0 redundant. Use z = ∑ θ_m φ_m, with φ_0 maybe x-0.5. But scale of θ controls deviation. Use softmax.

Low-dimensional optimization objective `score_fast(p)`. Use `scipy.optimize.minimize` method='L-BFGS-B' bounds maybe ±3 to avoid extreme, finite differences. Try many random θ within bounds. For each, evaluate and maybe continue.

But finite difference gradient for d=10 uses 20 evaluations per iteration; each eval conv n=1024 maybe 0.005s, 100 iterations=0.1s per start. Many starts easy. We can run low-dim for n=512/1024 with random restarts. Then full-dim refine.

However, global best might require high-dim features not captured. But low-dim can give good initial. We can also run full-dim from several initial shapes: linear a, quadratic, random smooth.

Now, full-dimensional optimizer details.

We need efficient gradient and line search. Let's define `np` global. The code cannot import evaluate_sequence. It can import numpy as np, time, maybe scipy.optimize.

Potential pitfalls:
- `np.fft` for many large arrays may be slower; choose convolution method based on n. For n≤2000, direct `np.convolve` may be faster than FFT? Actually direct C for 2500 O(6.25M) ~0.005? FFT with size 8192 ~0.002? Similar. For r amplitude, direct conv p_rev,w length n+2n ~3n=7500 direct ~18M, slower. Use FFT for both q and r with size power of 2.
- To compute q and r in one go? q uses p,p length Nq≥2n-1. r uses p_rev,w length Nr≥3n-2. Could use same N ≥3n-2 for both: FFT p to length N, q = ifft(|P|^2) first 2n-1; r = ifft(P_rev? Actually r_i = conv(p_rev,w). Need FFT of p_rev and w. We can compute P for p, P_rev for p_rev, W for w. That's 3 forward +2 inverse per iteration. For many iterations, costly. Maybe compute r via direct for n≤2048? Or use alternative formula with q and p? r_i =∑ p_j w_{i+j}. If w=softmax(βq) maybe w length 2n-1. Direct O(n^2) with numpy: can do via np.apply? Full optimizer:
```
def optimize_smooth(p0, n, time_limit, beta_initial=5, beta_final=200):
    p=p0.copy(); p/sum; history.
    best=p, best_score=fast_score(p)
    beta=beta_initial
    no_improve=0
    while time < limit:
        # for current beta do several iterations
        for it in range(max_iters_per_beta):
            q=fft_conv(p); m=q.max(); z=beta*(q-m); w=exp(z); w/=sum
            r=corr(p,w)
            d=p*r; d-=d.mean()
            if max(abs(d))<1e-15: break
            # compute candidates for eta
            # choose base_eta = target / (beta * max|d|) maybe target=0.5
            target=0.5
            base=target/(max(beta*1? ,...) * max(abs(d)))? Since exponent=eta*beta*d? Let's set exponent=eta*beta*d? Earlier 2βd. Use exponent=eta*beta*d. target=0.5 => base=target/(beta*maxd).
            for mult in [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1,3,10]:
                eta=base*mult
                cand=p*np.exp(-eta*beta*d)  # if exponent can large, clip
                cand=np.clip(cand,1e-15, None); cand/=sum
                s=fast_score(cand)
                if s<best_score: accept
        # Increase beta if converged
```

Let's design `gradient_direction(p, mode, beta, K)`:
```
q=fft_conv(p); m=q.max()
if mode=='soft': z=beta*(q-m); w=exp(z); w/=sum
else:
   idx = np.argpartition(q, -K)[-K:]  # top K
   w = np.zeros_like(q); w[idx]=1/K
r = corr(p,w)
d = p*r
d -= d.mean()
return d, q, m, score
```

Optimization loop details:
```
def optimize_full(p, deadline, max_iters, use_topk, initial_step=None):
    p=normalize(p.clip(min=1e-12))
    n=p.size
    best_score=fast_score(p); best=p.copy()
    current_score=best_score
    beta=5.0
    K=max(8, n//64)
    step=None
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and stall<...:
       d,q,m,score = get_direction(p, beta or topk)
       current_score=score # exact
       if score<best_score: update best
       if max(abs(d)) < 1e-14: # stationary
           # perturb: p *= exp(noise small), maybe if time
       if step is None: compute base_step = 1.0/(beta? or 10?)*? maybe from maxd.
       # try up to 8 steps: for mult in [1,0.3,0.1,0.03, -? no negative]
       accepted=False
       for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
           eta = step*mult
           cand = p * np.exp(-eta*d)  # for topk, d scale no beta; for soft maybe d already p*r, use eta*beta?
           # For consistency, direction d = p*r; update exponent = -eta*d (eta dimensionless). We can choose eta large.
```

`def _next_pow2(m):`
```
if m <=1: return 1
return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
Careful: `(m-1).bit_length()` works if m>0. For m=2n-1 maybe.

`def _normalize(p):`
```
s=p.sum()
if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return p
return p/s
```
But p might contain nan; handle.

`def _fast_convolution(p):`
```
n=p.size
m=2*n-1
N=_next_pow2(m)
P=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
q=np.fft.irfft(P*P, N)[:m]
# remove tiny neg
q[q<0 & q>-1e-12]=0? Use np.maximum(q,0)
return q
```
`P*P` is complex square. For real p. If p has float. Good.

`def _fast_corr_p_w(p, w):`
```
n=p.size
m=3*n-2
N=_next_pow2(m)
Pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
W=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
c=np.fft.irfft(Pr*W, N)
return c[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Check `w` length 2n-1. `rfft(w,N)` pads. Good.

`def _fast_score(p):`
```
p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12: return inf; p=p/s; q=_fast_convolution(p); return float(2*p.size*np.max(q))
```
We can avoid normalizing in fast_score? For search, p already normalized. But safe. It might alter if not. Use p copy? `p = np.asarray...` then normalized returns new; if later use p, need normalized. In optimizer, keep normalized. For score only, fine.

`def _score_with_q(p):` returns q, score.

`def _smooth_info(p, beta, k=None):`
```
q = _fast_convolution(p); m=q.max(); score=2*n*m
if beta is None or k:
   # topk weights
   if k is None: k=max(8, n//64)
   if k >= n? q length 2n-1; K top
   idx = np.argpartition(q, -K)[-K:]  # if K maybe > length? K up to 2n-1
   w=np.zeros_like(q); w[idx]=1.0/K
else:
   z=beta*(q-m)
   # if beta large, z range maybe; but m offset so max 0
   w=np.exp(z); wsum=w.sum(); if wsum==0: w uniform? else w/=wsum
r = _fast_corr_p_w(p,w)
d = p*r
d -= d.mean()
return d, q, m, score
```
`np.argpartition(q, -K)` requires K integer >0 and <= len(q). If K>=len, use all. For topk if K too large, choose all? We'll cap `K = min(K, q.size)`.
For softmax beta: if beta==0? not use. If beta*(q-m) min very negative, exp underflows to zero; okay wsum >0 because max index exp0. If m is nan? no.

`def _optimize_full(p0, deadline, max_no_improve, beta=20.0, top_k=None)`:
Need no closure. Use global? no.
```
p = np.asarray(p0, float).copy(); p = np.clip(p,1e-15,None); p=_normalize(p)
n=p.size
best=p.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(p)
eta = None
no_improve=0
last_score=best_score
while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < max_no_improve:
   d,q,m,score = _smooth_info(p, beta, top_k)
   if not np.isfinite(score): break
   if score < best_score - 1e-12:
       best_score=score; best=p.copy(); no_improve=0; last_score=score
   else:
       no_improve += 1
   maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
   if maxd < 1e-15:
       # stationary, perturb
       p = _perturb(p, rng, 0.1) # need rng pass
       continue
   if eta is None:
       target=0.5
       eta = target / maxd
   # Try multipliers. Step size may become huge; keep within [small, large]
   muls = (1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 0.3, 0.1, 0.03)
   accepted=False
   base_candidate = None
   base_candidate_score = score
   for mult in muls:
       step=eta*mult
       # ensure exponent not too large; if maxd*step > 5, skip/reduce
       max_exp = maxd*step
       if max_exp > 5.0: continue
       if max_exp < 1e-8: continue
       cand = p * np.exp(-step*d)
       cand = np.clip(cand, 1e-15, None)
       ssum = cand.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum<=0: continue
       cand /= ssum
       sc = _fast_score(cand)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=sc; best=cand.copy(); p=cand; no_improve=0; eta=step*2; accepted=True; break
       elif np.isfinite(sc) and sc < base_candidate_score:
           base_candidate_score=sc; base_candidate=cand
   if not accepted:
       if base_candidate is not None:
           # accept only if improves current best? maybe not current p? We want progress; if not best, could accept to escape? Maybe no.
           pass
       # reduce eta
       eta = max(eta*0.5, 1e-6/maxd if maxd>0 else eta)
       if no_improve > 50:
           # try perturbation and reset
           p=_perturb(p, rng, 0.05)
           no_improve=0
       elif no_improve > 100:
           break
return best,best_score
```

`_lowdim_objective` should be robust:
```
def _lowdim_objective(theta, n, basis):
    z = np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(basis.shape[0]): z += float(theta[i])*basis[i]
    z -= z.max()
    p = np.exp(z)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return 1e100
    p /= s
    return _fast_score(p)
```

Interpolation:
```
def _interp_shape(p, n_new, rng=None, noise=0.0):
    if p.size == n_new: return p.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,p.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    # interpolate log to new
    loin = np.interp(x_new, x_old, logp)
    if noise>0: loin += noise*rng.standard_normal(n_new)
    pnew = np.exp(loin - loin.max()); pnew /= pnew.sum()
    return pnew
```
If p has zeros, log -inf; max p. Use `np.maximum(p,1e-300)`. Good.

Shape generators:
```
def _linear_shape(n, a):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    y=x-0.5
    vals=1.0 + a*y
    vals=np.maximum(vals,1e-6)
    return _normalize(vals)
```
Positive for |a|<2.
`_poly_shape(n, coeffs)`: basis y,y^2,x(1-x),sin(pi x) etc. Use z = sum coeff*basis; p=softmax(z). For direct polynomial positive, use vals=1+...; but softmax easier.
```
def _poly_shape(n, a,b,c,d=0.0):
    y=np.linspace(0,1,n)-0.5
    z = a*y + b*y*y + c*(0.25-y*y) + d*np.sin(np.pi*(y+0.5))
    z-=z.max(); p=np.exp(z); return _normalize(p)
```
`_random_smooth_shape(n, rng, base_a=1.0)`:
```
K=4; z=base_a*(np.linspace(0,1,n)-0.5)
for m in range(1,5): amp=rng.uniform(0.0,0.8)/m; phase=rng.uniform(0,2pi); z += amp*np.sin(2pi*m*x + phase)
p=softmax(z)
```

Let's write `_active_direction`:
```
def _active_direction(p, k):
    n=p.size
    q=_fast_conv_p(p)
    m=float(np.max(q))
    score=float(2*n*m)
    L=q.size
    kk=int(min(max(k,1), L))
    idx=np.argpartition(q, -kk)[-kk:]
    vals=q[idx]
    s=vals.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w=np.full(L,1.0/L)
    else:
        w=np.zeros(L)
        w[idx]=vals/s
    r=_fast_conv_pw(p,w)
    d=p*r
    d=d-d.mean()
    return d,q,m,score
```
`np.argpartition` with kk==L: `argpartition(q,-L)` valid? It requires kth in [0,len-1], -L valid? -len is 0? Actually kth=-L equivalent 0? For full, `argpartition(q, -1)` gives all unsorted? If kk=L, just use `idx=np.arange(L)`. Handle.
If vals can be negative? q clipped nonneg, okay. If all zero (p zero), s=0.

For smooth variant, maybe use softmax with beta=100? But active enough.

`_optimize_full` step schedule. Need avoid infinite if eta huge. Let's implement more carefully:
```
def _optimize_full(p0, deadline, rng, k=None, max_no_improve=300):
    p=np.asarray(p0,float).copy()
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-15)
    p=_normalize(p)
    n=p.size
    if k is None: k=max(16,n//64)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(p)
    eta=None
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       d, q, m, score = _active_direction(p,k)
       if not np.isfinite(score): break
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best=p.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
       if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd < 1e-16:
           p=_perturb(p,rng,0.1)
           no_improve=0
           continue
       if eta is None:
           eta=1.0/maxd  # target 1.0? We'll use multipliers.
       # Ensure eta in reasonable relative: exponent max = eta*d; for mult 1 target 1.
       accepted=False
       # Try sequence of multipliers; larger first? Maybe [1,0.5,2,0.2,10,0.1]
       muls=(1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.2, 5.0, 0.1, 0.05)
       best_cand=None; best_cand_score=best_score
       for mult in muls:
           step=eta*mult
           max_exp=maxd*step
           if max_exp > 8.0 or max_exp < 1e-10: continue
           cand=p*np.exp(-step*d)
           cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-15)
           csum=cand.sum()
           if not np.isfinite(csum) or csum<=0: continue
           cand=cand/csum
           sc=_fast_score(cand)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
               best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand
       if best_cand is not None and best_cand_score < best_score - 1e-12:
           p=best_cand; best=p.copy(); best_score=best_cand_score; no_improve=0; eta=max(eta*1.5,1e-12)
       else:
           # If candidate improves current p but not best? best is current p because we only set p on best. So not.
           eta=max(eta*0.7, 1e-12/maxd if maxd>0 else eta)
           if no_improve > 30:
               # try larger step? maybe eta*3
               step=eta*10; max_exp=maxd*step
               if 1e-8 < max_exp < 8:
                  cand=...
                  if improved best: accept
               p=_perturb(p,rng,0.05)
               no_improve=0
           elif no_improve > 150:
               break
    p=_normalize(np.maximum(best,1e-15))
    return p,best_score
```
Using `best_cand_score < best_score` ensures only accept improvements over best. Since p=best always, okay. If a candidate makes score slightly worse but leads to future, not accepted; perturb may escape.

Potential issue: If current p not best (if we ever set p to non-best? We don't), fine.

We may want to use current score `score` from gradient, but if it differs from `_fast_score(p)` due normalize? Should same. Use best.

Step target: `eta=1/maxd` means mult=1 exponent max=1. Candidate maybe too aggressive; but line search evaluates several. Good.

If no improve, eta decays 0.7. If perturb resets? We don't change eta after perturb; maybe set eta based on new maxd next loop.

Add alternative direction: when no_improve between 10 and 30, compute `r` from active_direction? We only return d. Could modify `_active_direction` to also return r. But not necessary.

Maybe use top-K weighted by q; if all q equal (flat), d may be small; no improve. If score near lower bound, almost flat. Perturb.

Now, lowdim search implementation details:
```
def _lowdim_search(n, deadline, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    y=x-0.5
    basis=np.vstack((y, y*y, (x*(1-x)-0.25), np.sin(np.pi*x), np.cos(2*np.pi*x), np.sin(2*np.pi*x)))
    d=basis.shape[0]
    best_score=1e100; best_p=None
    starts=[]
    t_lin=np.zeros(d); t_lin[0]=1.4; starts.append(t_lin)
    t_lin2=np.zeros(d); t_lin2[0]=1.0; t_lin2[1]=-0.4; starts.append(t_lin2)
    for i in range(6):
       t=rng.normal(0,0.6,d); t[0]=abs(t[0])+0.5? ensure skew; starts.append(t)
    # time per start
    per_start = max(2.0,(deadline-time.monotonic()-1.0)/len(starts))
    # But scipy minimize no deadline. Limit maxiter: maxiter=int(min(120, max(20, per_start*20)))? Hard.
    for theta0 in starts:
       if _sp_opt is not None:
          bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)]*d
          maxiter=int(min(100, max(30, per_start*10)))
          try:
             res=_sp_opt.minimize(_lowdim_objective, theta0, args=(n,basis), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8})
             if res.fun<best_score:
                best_score=float(res.fun); z=basis.T.dot(res.x); z-=z.max(); p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum(); best_p=p
          except Exception:
             pass
       else:
          # simple coordinate for each start with limited time
          theta=np.array(theta0)
          sc=_lowdim_objective(theta,n,basis)
          if sc<best_score: update
          local_deadline = time.monotonic()+max(1.0,per_start*0.5)
          while time.monotonic()<local_deadline:
             improved=False
             for i in range(d):
                for delta in (0.2,-0.2,0.5,-0.5,0.1,-0.1):
                   tt=theta.copy(); tt[i]+=delta
                   s=_lowdim_objective(tt,n,basis)
                   if s<best_score:
                      best_score=s; theta=tt; update; improved=True
                      break? Need break inner. Use flags.
             if not improved: break
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline-5: break
    # also full optimize small for remaining 30s
    if best_p is None: best_p=_linear_shape(n,1.4)
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem>20:
       best_p,best_score=_optimize_full(best_p, time.monotonic()+min(rem*0.5,60), rng, k=max(8,n//64))
    return best_p,best_score
```
This code uses inner break but okay. However, derivative free coordinate fallback may be slow but limited.

In `_lowdim_search`, `basis` local, passed to objective as arg. Good.

But objective `_lowdim_objective` uses `_fast_score` which normalizes p. If p sum1, okay. It also computes score from length n. For lowdim n small, good.

One issue: `scipy.optimize.minimize` with `args=(n,basis)` where basis is 2D array. Each call passes same array. Okay.

No lambda? We use `bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)]*d`, okay.

Now, in `propose_candidate`, after lowdim small, we need manage deadlines. Let's write flow step by step.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0+max(0,float(budget_s)-5)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n_main=4096
    if budget_s < 600: n_main=1024? maybe.
    # Choose n_main based on budget
    if budget_s >= 2000: n_main=4096
    elif budget_s >= 800: n_main=2048
    else: n_main=1024
    small_n = min(256, n_main)
    best=None; best_score=np.inf
    # height_sequence
    try:
       h=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float)
       if h.ndim==1 and h.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
           ph=np.maximum(h,0); s=ph.sum()
           if s>0:
              ph=ph/s
              if ph.size != n_main: ph=_interp_shape(ph,n_main,rng,0.0)
              sc=_fast_score(ph)
              if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best=ph
    except Exception: pass
    # lowdim small if time
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
       small_deadline = min(deadline-900, time.monotonic()+120) # leave time
       small_p, small_score = _lowdim_search(small_n, small_deadline, rng)
       if small_p is not None:
          sp=_interp_shape(small_p,n_main,rng,0.05)
          sc=_fast_score(sp)
          if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best=sp
    # Build starts
    starts=[]
    if best is not None: starts.append(best)
    for a in [0.0,0.8,1.2,1.4,1.6,1.8, -1.0?]: starts.append(_linear_shape(n_main,a))
    # quadratic/poly random 20
    for i in range(20):
       a=rng.uniform(0.8,1.8); b=rng.uniform(-0.8,0.6); c=rng.uniform(-0.6,0.6); d=rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)
       starts.append(_poly_shape(n_main,a,b,c,d))
    # smooth random 20
    for i in range(20):
       starts.append(_smooth_shape(n_main,rng))
    # evaluate and sort
    scored=[]
    for idx,p in enumerate(starts):
       s=_fast_score(p)
       if np.isfinite(s): scored.append((s,idx))
    scored.sort()
    time_left = max(0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    # allocate to top starts
    fracs = (0.36,0.22,0.14,0.09,0.06,0.04,0.03,0.02,0.02,0.02)
    for j,(s,idx) in enumerate(scored[:len(fracs)]):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline-3: break
        frac=fracs[j]
        # last take remaining
        if j == len(fracs)-1:
            sub_deadline=deadline
        else:
            sub_deadline=time.monotonic() + time_left*frac
            # ensure not beyond deadline
            if sub_deadline > deadline: sub_deadline=deadline
        p_res,s_res = _optimize_full(starts[idx], sub_deadline, rng, k=max(32,n_main//64))
        if s_res<best_score:
            best_score=s_res; best=p_res
        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    # After main, if time remains and best_score >1.5027, try n_large
    if best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline-180 and best_score > 1.5027:
        n_large = 8192
        p_rem = _interp_shape(best,n_large,rng,0.02)
        sc_rem = _fast_score(p_rem)
        if sc_rem < best_score + 0.05: # allow maybe
            sub_deadline = deadline-3
            # maybe run with 70% remaining
            remaining=sub_deadline-time.monotonic()
            p2,s2=_optimize_full(p_rem, time.monotonic()+remaining*0.8, rng, k=max(64,n_large//64))
            if s2<best_score: best_score=s2; best=p2
    # Finalize
    if best is None:
        best=_linear_shape(1024,1.4)
    # Normalize and ensure float list
    best=np.maximum(best,0.0)
    s=best.sum()
    if s>0: best=best/s
    # Optionally validate with exact evaluator and if too high, fallback to previous best? evaluate_sequence maybe inf if issues.
    try:
        exact=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best])
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            if exact < best_score: best_score=exact
            # If exact is much worse due FFT underestimation, maybe we can still return best.
    except Exception:
        pass
    return [float(x) for x in best]
```
Need ensure `time_left` not stale after sub_deadline loops. We update.

However, if first start is best from lowdim with score maybe 1.6, optimizer might take 36% of time (~750s) and not improve much, leaving less for other starts. That's okay. Could use lower first frac 0.3. Let's use fracs=(0.30,0.20,0.15,0.10,0.08,0.06,0.05,0.04,0.02).


Let's write `_poly_shape` using softmax of z; but if we want direct linear maybe better. `_poly_shape`:
```
def _poly_shape(n,a,b,c,d):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=x-0.5
    z=a*y + b*y*y + c*(x*(1-x)-0.25) + d*np.sin(np.pi*x)
    z-=z.max(); p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum(); return p
```
This doesn't include constant; okay. If all coefficients zero, uniform.

`_linear_shape` could be `_poly_shape(n,a,0,0,0)` but direct linear p ensures shape exactly, not softmax. Let's keep direct for better baseline. Direct linear positive if |a|<2. Values range. Normalize.

`_smooth_shape`:
```
def _smooth_shape(n,rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    a=rng.uniform(-0.5,1.8)
    z=a*(x-0.5)
    for m in range(1,5):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.0,0.9)/m
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       z += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x+phase)
    z-=z.max(); p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum(); return p
```
Good.

```
            if not improved: break
```

```
def _direct_poly_shape(n,a,b,c,d=0.0):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=x-0.5
    vals=1+a*y+b*y*y+c*(x*(1-x)-0.25)+d*np.sin(np.pi*x)
    if np.min(vals) <= 1e-4: return None
    return _normalize(np.vstack? vals)
```

```
def _exact_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return inf; p=p/s; q=np.convolve(p,p); return float(2*p.size*max(q))
```

Let's refine `_optimize_full` to be efficient and robust:
```
def _optimize_full(p0, deadline, rng, k=None, exact=False, max_no_improve=200):
    p = ...
    if k is None: k=max(16,p.size//64)
    score_func = _exact_score if exact else _fast_score
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_func(p)
    eta=None; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        d, q, m, fscore = _active_direction(p,k)
        sc = score_func(p)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=sc; best=p.copy(); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if not finite or maxd<1e-15:
            p = _perturb(p,rng,0.08)
            no_improve = 0
            eta = None
            continue
        if eta is None:
            eta = 1.0 / maxd
        accepted = False
        # Try some steps. To reduce cost, try [1,0.5,2,0.25,4,0.1]
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25,4.0,0.1):
            step = eta*mult
            max_exp = maxd*step
            if max_exp < 1e-9 or max_exp > 6.0: continue
            cand = p * np.exp(-step*d)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-15)
            csum = cand.sum()
            if not finite or csum<=0: continue
            cand /= csum
            csc = score_func(cand)
            if finite(csc) and csc < best_score - 1e-12:
                 p=cand; best=cand.copy(); best_score=csc; no_improve=0; eta=step*1.3; accepted=True; break
        if not accepted:
            if no_improve > 80:
                p = _perturb(best.copy(), rng, 0.08)
                no_improve=0
                eta=None
            elif no_improve > 250:
                break
            else:
                eta = max(eta*0.75, 1e-8/maxd)
    return _normalize(np.maximum(best,0.0)), best_score
```
For exact final, score_func exact slow. Limit iterations by deadline.

```
def _perturb(p,rng,sigma):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).copy()
    n=p.size
    # Add smooth-ish noise: white plus low sine? Use white for escape.
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)*sigma
    # Add a bit smooth to avoid high freq: noise = 0.7*noise +0.3*lowpass? Simpler white.
    cand = p * np.exp(noise)
    return _normalize(np.maximum(cand,1e-15))
```

```python
"""
Search algorithm for low-peak squared cumulative step functions.

Strategy:
- Normalize sequences to sum 1 (score is scale invariant).
- Minimize 2n * max(conv(p,p)) with an FFT-based local search.
- Gradient direction is a weighted sum of active peak constraints
  (a soft active-set/log multiplicative update).
- Multi-resolution initialization with low-dimensional smooth shapes,
  polynomial grid starts, and random smooth starts.
- Final exact-convolution polish to guard against FFT rounding effects.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy import optimize as _sp_opt
except Exception:
    _sp_opt = None


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p
    return p / s


def _fast_conv_p(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(m)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, N)[:m]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    return q


def _fast_conv_p_rev_w(p, w):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    m = 3 * n - 2
    N = _next_pow2(m)
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Pr * W, N)
    return c[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def _fast_score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    p = p / s
    q = _fast_conv_p(p)
    return float(2.0 * p.size * float(np.max(q)))


def _exact_score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    p = p / s
    q = np.convolve(p, p)
    return float(2.0 * p.size * float(np.max(q)))


def _active_direction(p, k):
    n = int(p.size)
    q = _fast_conv_p(p)
    m = float(np.max(q))
    score = float(2.0 * n * m)
    L = int(q.size)
    kk = int(min(max(1, k), L))

    if kk >= L:
        vals = q.copy()
        s = float(np.sum(vals))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L))
        else:
            w = vals / s
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(q, -kk)[-kk:]
        vals = q[idx]
        s = float(np.sum(vals))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L))
        else:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
            w[idx] = vals / s

    r = _fast_conv_p_rev_w(p, w)
    d = p * r
    d = d - float(np.mean(d))
    return d, q, m, score


def _perturb(p, rng, sigma):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).copy()
    n = int(p.size)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = x - 0.5

    low = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=3)
    noise = (
        low[0] * y
        + low[1] * (x * (1.0 - x) - 0.25)
        + low[2] * np.sin(np.pi * x)
        + 0.15 * float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(n)
    )
    cand = p * np.exp(noise)
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-15)
    return _normalize(cand)


def _linear_shape(n, a):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    p = 1.0 + float(a) * (x - 0.5)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
    return _normalize(p)


def _direct_poly_shape(n, a, b, c, d=0.0):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    y = x - 0.5
    vals = (
        1.0
        + float(a) * y
        + float(b) * y * y
        + float(c) * (x * (1.0 - x) - 0.25)
        + float(d) * np.sin(np.pi * x)
    )
    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(vals, 1e-8)
    return _normalize(p)


def _p_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    if z.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return None
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return p / s


def _lowdim_objective(theta, n, basis):
    z = basis.T.dot(theta)
    p = _p_from_z(z)
    if p is None:
        return 1e100
    return _fast_score(p)


def _smooth_shape(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    a = rng.uniform(-0.5, 2.2)
    z = a * (x - 0.5)
    for m in range(1, 6):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0) / float(m)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        z += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase)
    p = _p_from_z(z)
    if p is None:
        p = _linear_shape(n, 1.5)
    return p


def _interp_shape(p, n_new, rng=None, noise=0.0):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    n_old = int(p.size)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return _normalize(p)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    loin = np.interp(x_new, x_old, lp)
    if noise > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        loin += float(noise) * rng.standard_normal(n_new)

    pnew = _p_from_z(loin)
    if pnew is None:
        pnew = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        pnew = np.interp(x_new, x_old, pnew)
        pnew = np.maximum(pnew, 1e-300)
    return _normalize(pnew)


def _lowdim_search(n, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = x - 0.5
    basis = np.vstack((
        y,
        y * y,
        x * (1.0 - x) - 0.25,
        np.sin(np.pi * x),
        np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        x,
    ))
    d = int(basis.shape[0])

    theta_starts = []

    t0 = np.zeros(d)
    t0[0] = 2.8
    theta_starts.append(t0)

    t1 = np.zeros(d)
    t1[0] = 2.6
    t1[1] = -0.5
    theta_starts.append(t1)

    t2 = np.zeros(d)
    t2[0] = 2.2
    t2[1] = -0.8
    t2[2] = -0.4
    theta_starts.append(t2)

    for _ in range(7):
        t = rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, size=d)
        t[0] = abs(t[0]) + 1.5
        theta_starts.append(t)

    best_score = 1e100
    best_p = None
    n_starts = len(theta_starts)
    remaining = max(1.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    per_start = remaining / float(max(1, n_starts))

    theta_bounds = [(-4.0, 4.0)] * d

    for theta0 in theta_starts:
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline) - 5.0:
            break

        if _sp_opt is not None:
            maxiter = int(min(55, max(20, per_start * 3.0)))
            try:
                res = _sp_opt.minimize(
                    _lowdim_objective,
                    np.array(theta0, dtype=float),
                    args=(n, basis),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=theta_bounds,
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-8},
                )
                if hasattr(res, "fun") and np.isfinite(res.fun) and res.fun < best_score:
                    best_score = float(res.fun)
                    z = basis.T.dot(res.x)
                    p = _p_from_z(z)
                    if p is not None:
                        best_p = p
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            theta = np.array(theta0, dtype=float)
            sc = _lowdim_objective(theta, n, basis)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                p = _p_from_z(basis.T.dot(theta))
                if p is not None:
                    best_p = p

            local_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, min(per_start * 0.6, 12.0))
            while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
                improved = False
                for i in range(d):
                    for delta in (0.25, -0.25, 0.6, -0.6, 0.1, -0.1):
                        tt = theta.copy()
                        tt[i] += delta
                        s2 = _lowdim_objective(tt, n, basis)
                        if s2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = s2
                            theta = tt
                            p = _p_from_z(basis.T.dot(theta))
                            if p is not None:
                                best_p = p
                            improved = True
                            break
                    if improved:
                        break
                if not improved:
                    break

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = _linear_shape(n, 1.7)
        best_score = _fast_score(best_p)

    rem = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 25.0:
        best_p, best_score = _optimize_full(
            best_p,
            time.monotonic() + min(rem * 0.45, 90.0),
            rng,
            k=max(16, n // 32),
            exact=False,
            max_no_improve=180,
        )

    return best_p, best_score


def _optimize_full(p0, deadline, rng, k=None, exact=False, max_no_improve=260):
    p = np.asarray(p0, dtype=float).copy()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-15)
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)

    if k is None or int(k) <= 0:
        k = max(32, n // 32)
    k = int(min(int(k), max(1, 2 * n - 1)))

    score_func = _exact_score if exact else _fast_score

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = float(score_func(p))
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    eta = None
    no_improve = 0
    deadline = float(deadline)

    if exact:
        muls = (1.0, 0.5, 2.0)
    else:
        muls = (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0, 0.1)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        d, q, m, _fscore = _active_direction(p, k)
        sc = float(score_func(p))

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if not np.isfinite(d.size):
            break
        maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd < 1e-15:
            p = _perturb(p, rng, 0.08)
            eta = None
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        if eta is None:
            eta = 1.0 / maxd

        cand = None
        cand_score = best_score

        for mult in muls:
            step = float(eta) * float(mult)
            max_exp = maxd * step
            if max_exp < 1e-9 or max_exp > 6.0:
                continue
            c = p * np.exp(-step * d)
            c = np.maximum(c, 1e-15)
            cs = float(np.sum(c))
            if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs <= 0.0:
                continue
            c = c / cs
            csc = float(score_func(c))
            if np.isfinite(csc) and csc < cand_score - 1e-12:
                cand_score = csc
                cand = c

        if cand is not None and cand_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            p = cand
            best = cand.copy()
            best_score = cand_score
            no_improve = 0
            step_used = max(1e-300, maxd * eta)
            eta = max(eta * 1.25, 1e-12)
        else:
            if no_improve > 90:
                p = _perturb(best.copy(), rng, 0.08)
                eta = None
                no_improve = 0
            elif no_improve > int(max_no_improve):
                break
            else:
                eta = max(float(eta) * 0.75, 1e-8 / maxd)

    best = np.where(np.isfinite(best), best, 0.0)
    best = np.maximum(best, 0.0)
    best = _normalize(best)
    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 6.0)
    deadline = t0 + total_budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if budget_s >= 2000:
        n_main = 4096
    elif budget_s >= 800:
        n_main = 2048
    else:
        n_main = 1024

    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Use provided previous construction if available.
    try:
        h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
        if h.ndim == 1 and h.size > 0:
            h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            hs = float(np.sum(h))
            if hs > 0.0:
                h = h / hs
                if h.size != n_main:
                    h = _interp_shape(h, n_main, rng, 0.0)
                sc = _fast_score(h)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_p = h
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Small-scale shape discovery.
    if time.monotonic() + 60.0 < deadline:
        small_deadline = min(deadline - max(120.0, total_budget * 0.35),
                             time.monotonic() + max(60.0, min(180.0, total_budget * 0.10)))
        if time.monotonic() < small_deadline - 10.0:
            small_n = min(256, n_main)
            try:
                small_p, _small_score = _lowdim_search(small_n, small_deadline, rng)
                if small_p is not None:
                    sp = _interp_shape(small_p, n_main, rng, 0.03)
                    sc = _fast_score(sp)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_p = sp
            except Exception:
                pass

    starts = []
    special = []

    if best_p is not None:
        starts.append(best_p.copy())
        special.append(0)

    # Linear baselines around the optimized continuous slope.
    for a in (1.30, 1.50, 1.60, 1.70, 1.72, 1.75, 1.80, 1.90):
        starts.append(_linear_shape(n_main, a))

    # Direct polynomial starts, including negative curvature terms.
    a_vals = (1.45, 1.55, 1.62, 1.68, 1.72, 1.76, 1.82)
    b_vals = (-0.55, -0.35, -0.20, -0.10, 0.00, 0.12)
    c_vals = (-0.45, -0.20, 0.00, 0.20)

    for a in a_vals:
        for b in b_vals:
            for c in c_vals:
                p = _direct_poly_shape(n_main, a, b, c)
                starts.append(p)
        p = _direct_poly_shape(n_main, a, b_vals[1], c_vals[0], d=0.25)
        starts.append(p)
        p = _direct_poly_shape(n_main, a, b_vals[0], c_vals[2], d=-0.25)
        starts.append(p)

    # Random smooth direct polynomial starts.
    for _ in range(35):
        a = rng.uniform(1.0, 2.1)
        b = rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.45)
        c = rng.uniform(-0.65, 0.55)
        d = rng.uniform(-0.45, 0.45)
        starts.append(_direct_poly_shape(n_main, a, b, c, d))

    # Random smooth spectral/phase starts.
    for _ in range(30):
        starts.append(_smooth_shape(n_main, rng))

    # If lowdim small phase produced a shape, make sure it gets a lane.
    try:
        if small_p is not None:
            sp = _interp_shape(small_p, n_main, rng, 0.02)
            starts.append(sp)
            special.append(len(starts) - 1)
    except Exception:
        pass

    scored = []
    for idx, p in enumerate(starts):
        s = _fast_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((s, idx))
    scored.sort()

    selected = []
    seen = set()
    for idx in special:
        if idx not in seen:
            selected.append(idx)
            seen.add(idx)

    for s, idx in scored:
        if len(selected) >= 10:
            break
        if idx not in seen:
            selected.append(idx)
            seen.add(idx)

    # Fallback if somehow empty.
    if not selected:
        selected = [len(starts)] - 1
        starts.append(_linear_shape(n_main, 1.72))
        selected = [len(starts) - 1]
        seen.add(selected[0])

    fracs = (0.28, 0.18, 0.13, 0.10, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05, 0.04, 0.04, 0.04)

    for j, idx in enumerate(selected):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 8.0:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem <= 0.0:
            break
        if j == len(fracs) - 1:
            sub_deadline = deadline
        else:
            sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + rem * fracs[j]
            if sub_deadline > deadline:
                sub_deadline = deadline

        try:
            cand, sc = _optimize_full(
                starts[idx],
                sub_deadline,
                rng,
                k=max(64, n_main // 32),
                exact=False,
                max_no_improve=240,
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = cand
        except Exception:
            pass

    # If the best is still above target and enough time remains, try a longer sequence.
    if (
        best_p is not None
        and best_score > 1.5027
        and time.monotonic() + 220.0 < deadline
    ):
        n_large = 8192
        try:
            large_p = _interp_shape(best_p, n_large, rng, 0.015)
            large_sc = _fast_score(large_p)
            if np.isfinite(large_sc):
                rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
                if rem > 120.0:
                    cand, sc = _optimize_full(
                        large_p,
                        time.monotonic() + max(60.0, rem * 0.80),
                        rng,
                        k=max(96, n_large // 24),
                        exact=False,
                        max_no_improve=240,
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_p = cand
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Exact-convolution polish.
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() + 20.0 < deadline:
        try:
            n_best = int(best_p.size)
            exact_time = 120.0 if n_best <= 4096 else 60.0
            sub_deadline = min(deadline - 2.0, time.monotonic() + exact_time)
            if sub_deadline > time.monotonic() + 8.0:
                cand, sc = _optimize_full(
                    best_p,
                    sub_deadline,
                    rng,
                    k=max(96, int(best_p.size) // 24),
                    exact=True,
                    max_no_improve=120,
                )
                esc = _exact_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(esc) and esc <= sc + 1e-9:
                    best_p = cand
                    best_score = min(best_score, esc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final validation with the exact convolver.
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = _linear_shape(2048, 1.72)

    best_p = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=float)
    best_p = np.where(np.isfinite(best_p), best_p, 0.0)
    best_p = np.maximum(best_p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(best_p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        best_p = _linear_shape(2048, 1.72)

    best_p = best_p / s

    try:
        es = _exact_score(best_p)
        if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_score:
            best_score = es
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_p]
```
